## The ticket

Give fine-tuning a single, honest step ledger: one definition of a training step, one checkpoint identity, and a resume contract that refuses a reshaped run.

Today `TinkerTrainer` counts `total_steps` in batches while it only takes an optimizer step every `gradient_accumulation_steps` batches (`tinker_trainer.py:271-272` vs `:334-338`), its learning-rate helper ignores `total_steps` (`:536-551`), its trailing accumulation window is flushed only under a `TINKER_AVAILABLE` guard (`:395-409`), resume rebuilds a position from `(resume_epoch - 1) * steps_per_epoch` with no record of the shape it was taken in (`:304-316`), and both the mock loop and `fireworks_trainer.py:435-446` reach for the global RNG, `time.sleep` and `time.time`. Fix all of it behind one new leaf module.

### New module `src/bespokelabs/curator/finetune/step_ledger.py`

- Exports the constants `STEP_UNIT_OPTIMIZER = "optimizer_step"`, `STEP_UNIT_PACKED = "packed_epoch_step"`, `DATASET_SIGNATURE_PREFIX = "ds1"`.
- Exports the exception family: `StepLedgerError`, which subclasses **`ValueError`**, and `ResumePlanMismatch(StepLedgerError)` carrying `checkpoint_name: str`, `mismatched_fields: Tuple[str, ...]`, `expected: dict`, `found: dict`.
- Exports the **frozen dataclasses** `StepPlan` and `ResumePlan` — not pydantic models, not `NamedTuple`s, not dicts.
- `StepPlan` fields, in this order: `step_unit`, `num_examples`, `batch_size`, `epochs`, `gradient_accumulation_steps`, `batches_per_epoch`, `total_batches`, `total_steps`, `trailing_window_batches`, `dataset_signature`.
- `ResumePlan` fields, in this order: `checkpoint_name`, `start_batch_ordinal`, `start_epoch`, `start_batch_in_epoch`, `completed_steps`, `remaining_batches`.
- The module is a leaf: it must import neither `time` nor `datetime` nor `random`, and any import of `finetune.types` or `finetune.config` must sit under `if TYPE_CHECKING:` so `TrainingResult.step_plan: Optional[StepPlan]` creates no cycle. `xxhash.xxh64` (already a direct dependency) is the only digest. No new dependency.

### The step unit: `plan_steps`

- `plan_steps(num_examples, *, batch_size, epochs, gradient_accumulation_steps=1, dataset_signature="") -> StepPlan`.
- The unit of `total_steps`, `warmup_steps`, `log_every_n_steps` and `checkpoint_every_n_steps` becomes the **optimizer step**, not the batch.
- `batches_per_epoch = ceil(num_examples / batch_size)`; `total_batches = batches_per_epoch * epochs`; `total_steps = ceil(total_batches / gradient_accumulation_steps)` — the accumulation window is counted over the **whole run** and is **not** reset at an epoch boundary, so a window may span one.
- `trailing_window_batches = total_batches - (total_steps - 1) * gradient_accumulation_steps`; a short final window still counts as one whole optimizer step.
- Methods (1-based batch ordinals in): `step_of_batch(b)`, `is_step_boundary(b)`, `epoch_of_batch(b)`, `batch_slice(b) -> (start, end)` into the example list, `logging_steps(log_every_n_steps)` (steps divisible by `n`), and `loss_history_steps(log_every_n_steps)` — the ascending, deduplicated union of the logging steps and, for each epoch, the step that closes the window containing that epoch's last batch.
- Raises `StepLedgerError` when `num_examples == 0`, and when any of `batch_size`, `epochs`, `gradient_accumulation_steps` is `< 1`.
- `plan_steps_for_config(config: "TinkerTrainerConfig", examples) -> StepPlan` builds the plan from config fields and the examples.
- `plan_packed_steps(num_examples, *, epochs, dataset_signature="") -> StepPlan` is the Fireworks unit — one packed step per epoch: `step_unit=STEP_UNIT_PACKED`, `batch_size=num_examples`, `gradient_accumulation_steps=1`, `batches_per_epoch=1`, `total_batches=total_steps=epochs`, `trailing_window_batches=1`; `StepLedgerError` on `num_examples == 0`.

### `dataset_signature`

- `dataset_signature(examples: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str` returns `f"ds1-{len(examples)}-{xxh64(payload).hexdigest()}"` where `payload = json.dumps(list(examples), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str, ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")`.
- It is a function of the content of every example in order: reordering or editing one character changes it; dict key order does not.

### Learning rate

- `learning_rate_at(step, total_steps, base_lr, warmup_steps) -> float` lives in the ledger and, unlike the code today, actually depends on `total_steps`.
- `TinkerTrainer._get_learning_rate(step, total_steps)` keeps its signature and delegates, passing `config.adam_params.learning_rate` and `config.warmup_steps`.
- `warmup_steps` now counts optimizer steps.

### `types.py` — appended, defaulted fields

- `CheckpointInfo` gains, appended in this order: `batch_size: int = 0`, `gradient_accumulation_steps: int = 0`, `batches_completed: int = 0`, `dataset_signature: str = ""`. `batches_completed` is the number of batches completed at that moment, counted globally across epochs.
- `TrainingStats` gains `current_batch: int = 0` (1-based ordinal just finished) and `total_batches: int = 0`; `current_step` now means completed optimizer steps and `total_steps` optimizer steps.
- `TrainingResult` gains `total_batches: int = 0` and `step_plan: Optional[StepPlan] = None`.
- Every existing construction site must keep working unchanged.

### `config.py`

- `TinkerTrainerConfig.seed` and `FireworksTrainerConfig.seed`, both `Field(default=0, ge=0)`.

### `TinkerTrainer.train`

- Build the plan first, then walk batches by their global 1-based ordinal `b`, calling `_training_step(..., should_optim_step=plan.is_step_boundary(b))`.
- The final batch of the run is a boundary, so the short trailing window is stepped **inside** the loop; delete the post-loop flush at `:395-409` with its `TINKER_AVAILABLE` guard and swallowing `except Exception`, so the flush happens in mock mode too.
- Push exactly one `TrainingStats` per batch, **after** that batch's optional optimizer step, with `current_batch = b`, `current_step` = optimizer steps completed so far (flat inside a window), `total_steps = plan.total_steps`, `total_batches = plan.total_batches`, `current_epoch = plan.epoch_of_batch(b)`, and the learning rate of the step that batch belongs to. Construct `FinetuneStatusTracker` with `total_steps=plan.total_steps`.
- `loss_history` gets exactly one entry per step in `plan.loss_history_steps(config.log_every_n_steps)`, ascending; each entry is the arithmetic mean of the per-batch losses in that step's accumulation window. `final_loss` is `loss_history[-1]`.
- `metadata` gains exactly two keys: `"gradient_accumulation_steps"`, valued `config.gradient_accumulation_steps`, and `"dataset_signature"`, valued `plan.dataset_signature`. The keys already there — `base_model`, `batch_size`, `learning_rate`, `lora_rank`, `lora_alpha` — keep their names and values.

### Checkpoints

- `save_checkpoint(self, name, step, epoch, loss, *, batch_size=0, gradient_accumulation_steps=0, batches_completed=0, dataset_signature="") -> Optional[CheckpointInfo]` records the shape the checkpoint was taken in and returns the record it stored.
- Checkpoints are written only at optimizer-step boundaries; `checkpoint_every_n_steps` and `checkpoint_every_epoch` keep their names and now speak in optimizer steps.
- A checkpoint's `name` is derived deterministically from `config.checkpoint_name_prefix` and the step, replacing the two shapes at `:363` and `:383`.

### Resume

- `load_checkpoint(checkpoint: CheckpointInfo) -> bool` stores the whole `CheckpointInfo` on `self._resume_from`, replacing `_resume_from_step`/`_resume_from_epoch`; `train()` clears it at the start, so a second `train()` starts from zero.
- `plan_resume(plan: StepPlan, checkpoint: "CheckpointInfo") -> ResumePlan` validates in a fixed order and raises `ResumePlanMismatch` out of `train()` — never a warning, never a silent restart:
  1. **shape** — `batch_size`, `dataset_signature`, `gradient_accumulation_steps` against the plan; every differing name goes into `mismatched_fields`, **sorted alphabetically** (a pre-ledger checkpoint fails all three);
  2. **exhaustion** — `checkpoint.batches_completed >= plan.total_batches` ⇒ `mismatched_fields == ("epochs",)`;
  3. **consistency** — `checkpoint.step != plan.step_of_batch(checkpoint.batches_completed)` ⇒ `mismatched_fields == ("step",)`.
- Otherwise the run restarts at the batch **after** the checkpoint: `start_batch_ordinal = checkpoint.batches_completed` (0-based), `start_epoch = batches_completed // batches_per_epoch + 1`, `start_batch_in_epoch = batches_completed % batches_per_epoch`, `completed_steps = checkpoint.step`, `remaining_batches = total_batches - batches_completed`.
- Raising `epochs` alone is a legal resume: the plan grows and the schedule continues from `completed_steps + 1`.

### Determinism

- Both trainers take keyword-only `clock: Optional[Callable[[], float]] = None` and `rng: Optional[random.Random] = None`; `self._clock = clock if clock is not None else time.time`, `self._rng = rng if rng is not None else random.Random(config.seed)`.
- The mock branch of `_training_step` draws exactly one `self._rng.random()` per batch, in batch order, and computes `2.5 - draw * 0.5`. Delete `time.sleep(0.01)` at `:488` and at `fireworks_trainer.py:439`.
- `TinkerTrainer.train` calls `self._clock()` exactly twice on a run that saves no weights: once before the loop, once for `total_time`. Under `save_weights_on_complete=True` it calls it a **third** time, after `total_time`, for the auto-name at `:420` — `self.save_weights(f"{config.base_model}_lora_{int(self._clock())}")` — a fresh reading, never the value `total_time` was computed from. `get_sampling_client`'s `int(time.time())` at `:596` is on the real-SDK path and stays.
- No module-level `random` or `time.time` call may remain on any mock path.

### Fireworks

- `FireworksTrainer.train` (`:289`) and `_mock_train` (`:444`) build `plan_packed_steps` and set `total_steps=plan.total_steps`, `total_batches`, `step_plan=plan` instead of `total_steps=0`; both metadata dicts gain exactly one key, `"step_unit"`, valued `"packed_epoch_step"`.

`data_formatter.py`, `status_tracker.py`, `base_trainer.py` and `fireworks_data_formatter.py` do not change. Python `^3.10`; `tinker` is not installed in the test environment, so every trainer runs its mock branch.

## Requirements settled earlier

These were agreed before the ticket was written. They are not optional.

### r1

- **rule** — `CheckpointInfo` gains a last field `reasons: Tuple[str, ...] = ()`, and `save_checkpoint` gains a keyword-only `reasons: Sequence[str] = ("interval",)`. The vocabulary is exactly three strings held in a module constant `CHECKPOINT_REASONS: Tuple[str, str, str] = ("interval", "epoch", "final")` in `step_ledger.py`, and `canonical_reasons(reasons: Sequence[str]) -> Tuple[str, ...]` deduplicates and orders them by that ledger order. A step that fires several triggers produces exactly one `save_checkpoint` call whose `reasons` is that canonical tuple. The stored `name` comes from `checkpoint_name(prefix, step)` = `CHECKPOINT_NAME_TEMPLATE.format(prefix=..., step=...)` with `CHECKPOINT_NAME_TEMPLATE = "{prefix}-s{step:06d}"`: `checkpoint_name("checkpoint", 2) == "checkpoint-s000002"`.
- **scope** — The `"final"` trigger fires at `step == plan.total_steps` regardless of configuration. The `"interval"` and `"epoch"` triggers remain conditional on their config fields. Applies to `TinkerTrainer` only; `FireworksTrainer` writes no checkpoints and gains no reasons.
- **exclusions_or_crossover** — The `epoch` recorded on a checkpoint is `plan.epoch_of_batch(checkpoint.batches_completed)`, never the `for epoch in range(...)` loop variable. A checkpoint whose reasons include `"epoch"` for the end of epoch 1 therefore records `epoch == 2`.
- **failure_behavior** — `canonical_reasons` raises `StepLedgerError` on any string outside `CHECKPOINT_REASONS`. A repeat `save_checkpoint` under a name equal to the last entry of `self._checkpoints` replaces that entry in place — merging both reason tuples through `canonical_reasons` and taking the new `loss`: `save_checkpoint("checkpoint-s000002", step=2, epoch=2, loss=0.5, reasons=("interval",))` then the same name with `loss=0.25, reasons=("final",)` leaves `len(trainer.get_checkpoints()) == 1`, `reasons == ("interval", "final")`, `loss == 0.25`.
- **observability** — For the end-to-end run (10 examples, `batch_size=3`, `epochs=2`, `gradient_accumulation_steps=3`, `checkpoint_every_n_steps=2`, `checkpoint_every_epoch=True`, `seed=0`): `[c.name for c in result.checkpoints] == ["checkpoint-s000002", "checkpoint-s000003"]`, `[c.reasons for c in result.checkpoints] == [("interval", "epoch"), ("epoch", "final")]`, and `[(c.step, c.epoch, c.batches_completed) for c in result.checkpoints] == [(2, 2, 6), (3, 2, 8)]`. `[f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(CheckpointInfo)]` ends `[..., "dataset_signature", "reasons"]` with length 10 and `CheckpointInfo(name="a", path="b", step=1, epoch=1, loss=0.5).reasons == ()`. The default-config fixture of `tests/finetune/test_trainer.py:19-46` yields exactly one checkpoint, `"checkpoint-s000001"`, with `reasons == ("final",)`.

### r2

- **rule** — `learning_rate_at(step, total_steps, base_lr, warmup_steps, *, min_lr_ratio: float = MIN_LR_RATIO) -> float` where `MIN_LR_RATIO: float = 0.1` is a module constant of `step_ledger.py`. With `effective_warmup = min(warmup_steps, total_steps)`, for `1 <= step <= effective_warmup` the rate is `base_lr * step / effective_warmup`. After warmup the rate decays: `progress = min((step - effective_warmup) / max(total_steps - effective_warmup, 1), 1.0)` and `lr = base_lr * (1.0 - (1.0 - min_lr_ratio) * progress)`.
- **exclusions_or_crossover** — Beyond the end of the run the rate is clamped at the floor and does not fall below it or go negative: `learning_rate_at(99, 8, 1e-4, 2) == pytest.approx(1e-05)`. A resumed run whose `epochs` grew continues down the new, longer schedule from `completed_steps + 1` using the same function.
- **failure_behavior** — `warmup_steps` greater than `total_steps` is clipped to the run length rather than raising: `[learning_rate_at(s, 3, 1e-4, 10) for s in range(1, 4)] == pytest.approx([1e-4/3, 2e-4/3, 1e-04], rel=1e-12)` — ending exactly at `base_lr`.
- **observability** — `[learning_rate_at(s, 8, 1e-4, 2) for s in range(1, 9)] == pytest.approx([5e-05, 1e-04, 8.5e-05, 7e-05, 5.5e-05, 4e-05, 2.5e-05, 1e-05], rel=1e-12)`; `[learning_rate_at(s, 4, 1e-4, 0) for s in range(1, 5)] == pytest.approx([7.75e-05, 5.5e-05, 3.25e-05, 1e-05], rel=1e-12)`. In the end-to-end run the three optimizer steps carry `5e-05`, `1e-04`, `1e-05`, and those are the values pushed on `TrainingStats.learning_rate` for the batches of each window.

</problem>

Final loss: {final_loss:.4f}, Time: {total_time:.2f}s\\")\\n        return result\\n\\n    def _training_step(self, batch_data: List[Any], learning_rate: float, should_optim_step: bool = True) -> tuple:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Execute a single training step (forward-backward, and optionally optimizer step).\\n\\n        Args:\\n            batch_data: List of Tinker Datum objects or formatted examples\\n            learning_rate: Current learning rate for this step\\n            should_optim_step: Whether to run the optimizer step (False when accumulating gradients)\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Tuple of (loss, tokens_processed)\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self._training_client is not None and TINKER_AVAILABLE:\\n            try:\\n                fwd_bwd_future = self._training_client.forward_backward(batch_data, loss_fn=\\"cross_entropy\\")\\n                fwd_bwd_result = fwd_bwd_future.result()\\n\\n                loss_sum = fwd_bwd_result.metrics.get(\\"loss:sum\\", 0.0)\\n                total_tokens = self._get_batch_token_count(batch_data)\\n                effective_target_weight = self._get_effective_target_weight(batch_data)\\n                avg_loss = loss_sum / max(effective_target_weight, 1.0)\\n\\n                if should_optim_step:\\n                    adam_params = tinker.AdamParams(\\n                        learning_rate=learning_rate,\\n                        beta1=self.config.adam_params.beta1,\\n                        beta2=self.config.adam_params.beta2,\\n                        eps=self.config.adam_params.epsilon,\\n                        weight_decay=self.config.adam_params.weight_decay,\\n                    )\\n                    optim_future = self._training_client.optim_step(adam_params)\\n                    optim_future.result()  # Wait for completion\\n\\n                return avg_loss, total_tokens\\n\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                logger.warning(f\\"Training step failed: {e}. mock_tokens == 0:\\n            mock_tokens = len(batch_data) * 100\\n\\n        time.sleep(0.01)\\n\\n        return mock_loss, mock_tokens\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def if hasattr(datum, \\"model_input\\") and getattr(datum.model_input, \\"chunks\\", None):\\n                total_tokens += len(datum.model_input.chunks[0].tokens)\\n            elif isinstance(datum, float:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Count the weighted target tokens that contribute to loss.\\"\\"\\"\\n        effective_weight = 0.0\\n        found_weights = False\\n\\n        for datum in batch_data:\\n weight in weights)\\n\\n        if found_weights and effective_weight > 0:\\n            return effective_weight\\n\\n        return float(TinkerTrainer._get_batch_token_count(batch_data))\\n\\n    def _get_learning_rate(self, step: int, total_steps: int) -> float:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Calculate learning rate with optional warmup.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            step: Current training step\\n            total_steps: Total number of training steps\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Current learning rate\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        base_lr = self.config.adam_params.learning_rate\\n\\n        if self.config.warmup_steps > 0 and step < self.config.warmup_steps:\\n            return base_lr * (step / self.config.warmup_steps)\\n\\n        return base_lr\\n\\n    def save_weights(self, name: str) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Save the trained LoRA weights.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            name: Name for the saved weights\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Identifier for the saved weights\\n self._sampling_client\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                logger.warning(f\\"Failed to create sampling client: {e}\\")\\n\\n        return None\\n\\n    def sample(\\n        self,\\n        prompt: str,\\n        sampling_config: Optional[SamplingConfig] = None,\\n        system_prompt: Optional[str] = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Generate a sample from the fine-tuned model.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            prompt: The user prompt\\n            sampling_config: Optional sampling configuration\\n            system_prompt: Optional system prompt\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Generated text\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if sampling_config is None:\\n            sampling_config = SamplingConfig()\\n\\n        messages: List[ChatMessage] = []\\n        if system_prompt:\\n            messages.append(ChatMessage(role=\\"system\\", content=system_prompt))\\n        messages.append(ChatMessage(role=\\"user\\", content=prompt))\\n\\n tinker.ModelInput(chunks=[tinker.EncodedTextChunk(tokens=prompt_tokens)])\\n                sample_params = tinker.SamplingParams(\\n                    max_tokens=sampling_config.max_tokens,\\n -1,\\n                    stop=sampling_config.stop_sequences if sampling_config.stop_sequences else None,\\n                )\\n                sample_result = sampling_client.sample(prompt_input, This trainer wraps that flow behind the same\\n:class:`BaseTrainer` interface used by the rest of the ``finetune`` module.\\n\\nThe official Fireworks \\"build SDK\\" (``pip install fireworks-ai``) is used for the\\ncontrol plane (dataset upload, job submission, deployment), and the OpenAI-compatible\\ninference endpoint is used for sampling. When the SDK is not installed or no API key\\nis available, the trainer runs in \\"mock mode\\" so examples and tests work offline.\\n\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nimport inspect\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport tempfile\\nimport time\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\\n\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.finetune.config import FireworksTrainerConfig\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.finetune.fireworks_data_formatter import FireworksDataFormatter\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.finetune.trainer.base_trainer import BaseTrainer\\nfrom _fireworks\\n        FIREWORKS_AVAILABLE = True\\n    else:\\n        FIREWORKS_AVAILABLE = False\\nexcept Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - optional dep; never let a broken install break curator import\\n    FIREWORKS_AVAILABLE = False\\n\\n\\n# Terminal job states reported by the Fireworks API (normalized JobState names; a\\n# leading \\"JOB_STATE_\\" prefix from the REST API is stripped before comparison).\\n# Intermediate states (CREATING, RUNNING, VALIDATING, WRITING_RESULTS, PENDING,\\n# EVALUATION, ROLLOUT, etc.) are not listed here and simply continue polling.\\n# Default accelerator for on-demand inference deployments when none is configured.\\n_DEFAULT_DEPLOY_ACCELERATOR = \\"NVIDIA_H100_80GB\\"\\n\\n# Fireworks JobState enum values (the SDK stores `_proto.state` as a raw int).\\n_JOB_STATE_NAMES_BY_VALUE = {\\n    0: \\"UNSPECIFIED\\",\\n    1: \\"CREATING\\",\\n    2: \\"RUNNING\\",\\n    3: \\"COMPLETED\\",\\n    4: \\"FAILED\\",\\n    5: \\"CANCELLED\\",\\n    6: \\"DELETING\\",\\n    7: \\"WRITING_RESULTS\\",\\n    8: \\"VALIDATING\\",\\n    9: \\"ROLLOUT\\",\\n    10: \\"EVALUATION\\",\\n    11: \\"FAILED_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n    12: \\"DELETING_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n    13: \\"POLICY_UPDATE\\",\\n    14: \\"PENDING\\",\\n    15: \\"EXPIRED_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n    16: \\"EXPIRED\\",\\n    17: \\"CREATING_DEPENDENCIES\\",\\n    18: \\"RE_QUEUEING\\",\\n    19: \\"CREATING_INPUT_DATASET\\",\\n}\\n\\n_COMPLETED_STATES = {\\"COMPLETED\\"}\\n_FAILED_STATES = {\\n    \\"FAILED\\",\\n    \\"FAILED_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n    \\"CANCELLED\\",\\n    \\"DELETING\\",\\n    \\"DELETING_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n    \\"EXPIRED\\",\\n    \\"EXPIRED_CLEANING_UP\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\nclass FireworksTrainer(BaseTrainer):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Trainer that uses the Fireworks AI managed fine-tuning API.\\n\\n    Example usage:\\n        ```python\\n        from bespokelabs.curator import FireworksTrainer, FireworksTrainerConfig\\n        from datasets import Dataset\\n\\n        data = [\\n            {\\"messages\\": [\\n                {\\"role\\": \\"user\\", \\"content\\": \\"What is Python?\\"},\\n                {\\"role\\": \\"assistant\\", \\"content\\": \\"Python is a programming language.\\"}\\n            ]},\\n        ]\\n        dataset = Dataset.from_list(data * 3)  # Fireworks requires >= 3 examples\\n\\n        config = FireworksTrainerConfig(\\n            base_model=\\"qwen3-4b\\",\\n            epochs=1,\\n            lora_rank=8,\\n        )\\n\\n        trainer = FireworksTrainer(config)\\n        result = trainer.train(dataset)        # uploads data, runs the SFT job\\n        response = trainer.sample(\\"Explain recursion\\")  # deploys + runs inference\\n        trainer.close()                        # tears down the deployment (optional)\\n        ```\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: FireworksTrainerConfig):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize the FireworksTrainer.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            config: FireworksTrainerConfig with training parameters.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.config = config\\n        self.data_formatter = FireworksDataFormatter()\\n        self._output_model: Optional[str] = None\\n        self._job_name: Optional[str] = None\\n        self._job_url: Optional[str] = None\\n        self._is_trained = False\\n\\n        # Populated when the SDK and an API key are available.\\n        self._base_llm: Optional[Any] = None\\n        self._account_id: Optional[str] = config.account_id\\n        self._deployment_llm: Optional[Any] = None\\n        self._deployment_ready = False\\n\\n        self._initialize_client()\\n\\n    @property\\n    def is_mock(self) -> bool:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Whether the trainer is running in mock mode (no SDK/key).\\"\\"\\"\\n        return self._base_llm is None\\n\\n    def _initialize_client(self) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize the Fireworks base-model handle used to launch SFT jobs.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if not self.config.api_key:\\n            logger.warning(\\"No FIREWORKS_API_KEY provided. Running in mock mode.\\")\\n            return\\n\\n        if not FIREWORKS_AVAILABLE:\\n            logger.warning(\\"Fireworks SDK not installed. Running in mock mode. meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "\\"\\"\\"Abstract base class for trainers.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict\\n\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.finetune.types import TrainingExample, TrainingResult\\n\\n\\nclass BaseTrainer(ABC):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Abstract base class for fine-tuning trainers.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def train(self, dataset: Any) -> TrainingResult:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Run training on the provided dataset.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            dataset: The training dataset (HuggingFace Dataset or similar)\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            TrainingResult with training metrics and metadata\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def save_weights(self, name: str) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Save the trained weights.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            name: Name for the saved weights\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Identifier or path to the saved weights\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def format_example(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> TrainingExample:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Format a dataset row into a TrainingExample.\\n\\n        Override this method to customize how your data is formatted.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            row: A dictionary containing the training data\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            TrainingExample with formatted messages\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def sample(self, prompt: str, **kwargs) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Generate a sample from the trained model.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            prompt: The input prompt\\n            **kwargs: Additional sampling parameters\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Generated text\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "\\"\\"\\"Type definitions for fine-tuning module.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\\n\\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\\n\\n\\nclass ChatMessage(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"A single chat message.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    role: str\\n    content: str\\n\\n\\nclass TrainingExample(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"A single training example with chat messages.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    messages: List[ChatMessage]\\n\\n    @classmethod\\n    def from_dict_messages(cls, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> \\"TrainingExample\\":\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create a TrainingExample from a list of message dictionaries.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return cls(messages=[ChatMessage(**msg) for msg in messages])\\n\\n\\nclass SamplingConfig(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Configuration for inference/sampling.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\\"forbid\\")\\n\\n    max_tokens: int = 512\\n    temperature: float = 0.7\\n    top_p: float = 0.9\\n    top_k: int = 50\\n    stop_sequences: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass TrainingStats:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Real-time training statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    current_epoch: int = 0\\n    total_epochs: int = 0\\n    current_step: int = 0\\n    total_steps: int = 0\\n    current_loss: float = 0.0\\n    tokens_processed: int = 0\\n    samples_processed: int = 0\\n    learning_rate: float = 0.0\\n    elapsed_time: float = 0.0\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass CheckpointInfo:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Information about a saved checkpoint.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    name: str\\n    path: str\\n    step: int\\n    epoch: int\\n    loss: float\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass TrainingResult:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Result from a training run.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    final_loss: float\\n    total_steps: int\\n    total_epochs: int\\n    total_time: float\\n    tokens_processed: int\\n    samples_processed: int\\n    loss_history: List[float] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    weights_name: Optional[str] = None\\n    checkpoints: List[CheckpointInfo] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "\\"\\"\\"Configuration models for fine-tuning.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator\\n\\n\\nclass AdamParams(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Adam optimizer parameters.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\\"forbid\\")\\n\\n    learning_rate: float = Field(default=1e-4, gt=0)\\n    beta1: float = Field(default=0.9, ge=0, le=1)\\n    beta2: float = Field(default=0.999, ge=0, le=1)\\n    weight_decay: float = Field(default=0.01, ge=0)\\n    epsilon: float = Field(default=1e-8, gt=0)\\n\\n\\nclass LoRAConfig(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"LoRA-specific configuration.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\\"forbid\\")\\n\\n    rank: int = Field(default=16, gt=0)\\n    alpha: int = Field(default=32, gt=0)\\n    dropout: float = Field(default=0.05, ge=0, le=1)\\n    target_modules: list = Field(default_factory=lambda: [\\"q_proj\\", \\"v_proj\\", \\"k_proj\\", \\"o_proj\\"])\\n\\n\\nclass TinkerTrainerConfig(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Main configuration for TinkerTrainer.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        base_model: Name of the base model to fine-tune (e.g., \\"Qwen/Qwen3-8B\\")\\n        epochs: Number of training epochs\\n        batch_size: Training batch size\\n        max_seq_length: Maximum sequence length for training\\n        adam_params: Adam optimizer parameters\\n        lora_config: LoRA-specific configuration\\n        api_key: Tinker API key (defaults to TINKER_API_KEY env var)\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps: Number of gradient accumulation steps\\n        warmup_steps: Number of warmup steps for learning rate scheduler\\n        log_every_n_steps: Log training stats every N steps\\n        save_weights_on_complete: Whether to save weights when training completes\\n        checkpoint_every_n_steps: Save checkpoint every N steps (0 to disable)\\n        checkpoint_every_epoch: Save checkpoint at the end of each epoch\\n        checkpoint_name_prefix: Prefix for checkpoint names\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    base_model: str\\n    epochs: int = Field(default=3, gt=0)\\n    batch_size: int = Field(default=4, gt=0)\\n    max_seq_length: int = Field(default=2048, gt=0)\\n    adam_params: AdamParams = Field(default_factory=AdamParams)\\n    lora_config: LoRAConfig = Field(default_factory=LoRAConfig)\\n    api_key: Optional[str] = None\\n    gradient_accumulation_steps: int = Field(default=1, gt=0)\\n    warmup_steps: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)\\n    log_every_n_steps: int = Field(default=10, gt=0)\\n    save_weights_on_complete: bool = True\\n    checkpoint_every_n_steps: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)\\n    checkpoint_every_epoch: bool = False\\n    checkpoint_name_prefix: str = \\"checkpoint\\"\\n\\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\\"forbid\\")\\n\\n    def __init__(self, **data):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize config, loading API key from environment if not provided.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if \\"api_key\\" not in data or data[\\"api_key\\"] is None:\\n            data[\\"api_key\\"] = os.environ.get(\\"TINKER_API_KEY\\")\\n        super().__init__(**data)\\n\\n\\n_VALID_LORA_RANKS = (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32)\\n_RESOURCE_NAME_RE = re.compile(r\\"^[a-z0-9-]+$\\")\\n\\n\\nclass FireworksTrainerConfig(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Configuration for FireworksTrainer (Fireworks AI managed fine-tuning).\\n\\n    Fireworks runs supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as a managed, server-side job: a\\n    chat-format JSONL dataset is uploaded, an SFT job is submitted against a base\\n    model, and the result is a new (LoRA) model that is served via an on-demand\\n    deployment. This mirrors :class:`TinkerTrainerConfig` but uses the parameters\\n    that the Fireworks API actually exposes.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        base_model: Base model to fine-tune. Either a short id (e.g. ``\\"qwen3-4b\\"``)\\n            or a fully-qualified id (``\\"accounts/fireworks/models/qwen3-4b\\"``).\\n        epochs: Number of training epochs (``examples * epochs`` must be <= 3,000,000).\\n            Note: Fireworks packs examples into batches by token count, so a small\\n            dataset may be a single batch (one optimizer step) per epoch. For tiny\\n            datasets, use many epochs so the LoRA gets enough gradient steps to learn.\\n        learning_rate: Learning rate. ``None`` lets Fireworks auto-select per base\\n            model (recommended).\\n        lora_rank: LoRA rank; must be a power of two up to 32. ``None`` requests a\\n            full-parameter fine-tune instead of LoRA.\\n        batch_size: Training batch size (tokens packed per step). ``None`` uses the\\n            Fireworks default.\\n        max_context_length: Maximum context length during training. ``None`` uses\\n            the base-model default.\\n        early_stop: Whether to enable early stopping.\\n        output_model: Desired id for the resulting fine-tuned model. ``None`` lets\\n            Fireworks derive it from the job id.\\n        display_name: Human-readable job name; must match ``^[a-z0-9-]+$``.\\n        api_key: Fireworks API key (defaults to the ``FIREWORKS_API_KEY`` env var).\\n        account_id: Fireworks account id. ``None`` auto-discovers it from the key.\\n        deployment_type: Deployment strategy used to serve the fine-tuned model for\\n            inference. Fine-tuned LoRA models cannot be served serverlessly, so this\\n            defaults to ``\\"on-demand\\"`` (Live Merge).\\n        accelerator_type: Optional accelerator override for the inference deployment.\\n        accelerator_count: Optional accelerator count for the inference deployment.\\n        auto_deploy: If True, :meth:`FireworksTrainer.sample` provisions an on-demand\\n            deployment automatically on first use. Always release it afterwards with\\n            :meth:`FireworksTrainer.close` or by using the trainer as a context manager.\\n        poll_interval_seconds: How often to poll the fine-tuning job for progress.\\n        max_wait_seconds: Maximum time to wait for a fine-tuning job to complete.\\n        inference_base_url: OpenAI-compatible base URL for inference.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    base_model: str\\n    epochs: int = Field(default=1, gt=0)\\n    learning_rate: Optional[float] = Field(default=None, gt=0)\\n    lora_rank: Optional[int] = Field(default=8)\\n    batch_size: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, gt=0)\\n    max_context_length: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, gt=0)\\n    early_stop: Optional[bool] = None\\n    output_model: Optional[str] = None\\n    display_name: str = \\"curator-sft\\"\\n    api_key: Optional[str] = None\\n    account_id: Optional[str] = None\\n\\n    # Inference / deployment\\n    deployment_type: str = \\"on-demand\\"\\n    accelerator_type: Optional[str] = None\\n    accelerator_count: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, gt=0)\\n    auto_deploy: bool = True\\n\\n    # Polling / inference\\n    poll_interval_seconds: int = Field(default=10, gt=0)\\n    max_wait_seconds: int = Field(default=3600, gt=0)\\n    inference_base_url: str = \\"https://api.fireworks.ai/inference/v1\\"\\n\\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\\"forbid\\")\\n\\n    @field_validator(\\"lora_rank\\")\\n    @classmethod\\n    def _validate_lora_rank(cls, value: Optional[int]) -> Optional[int]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Ensure lora_rank is a power of two up to 32 (Fireworks constraint).\\"\\"\\"\\n        if value is not None and value not in _VALID_LORA_RANKS:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"lora_rank must be one of {_VALID_LORA_RANKS} or None for full fine-tuning, got {value}\\")\\n        return value\\n\\n    @field_validator(\\"display_name\\")\\n    @classmethod\\n    def _validate_display_name(cls, value: str) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Ensure display_name only contains lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if not _RESOURCE_NAME_RE.fullmatch(value):\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"display_name must match ^[a-z0-9-]+$ (lowercase a-z, 0-9, hyphen), got {value!r}\\")\\n        return value\\n\\n    def __init__(self, **data):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize config, loading API key from environment if not provided.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if data.get(\\"api_key\\") is None:\\n            data[\\"api_key\\"] = os.environ.get(\\"FIREWORKS_API_KEY\\")\\n        super().__init__(**data)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def qualified_base_model(self) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Return the fully-qualified base model id (``accounts/fireworks/models/...``).\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.base_model.startswith(\\"accounts/\\"):\\n            return self.base_model\\n        return f\\"accounts/fireworks/models/{self.base_model}\\"",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "\\"\\"\\"Status tracker for training progress display.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nimport time\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\nimport tqdm\\nfrom rich import box\\nfrom rich.console import Console\\nfrom rich.live import Live\\nfrom rich.panel import Panel\\nfrom rich.progress import BarColumn, Progress, TextColumn, TimeElapsedColumn, TimeRemainingColumn\\nfrom rich.table import Table\\n\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.finetune.types import TrainingStats\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.log import _CONSOLE, USE_RICH_DISPLAY\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.status_tracker.tqdm_constants.colors import END, HEADER, METRIC, MODEL\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass FinetuneStatusTracker:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Tracks and displays fine-tuning progress.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    model: str = \\"\\"\\n    total_epochs: int = 0\\n    total_steps: int = 0\\n    batch_size: int = 1\\n\\n    # Current stats\\n    current_epoch: int = 0\\n    current_step: int = 0\\n    current_loss: float = 0.0\\n    tokens_processed: int = 0\\n    samples_processed: int = 0\\n    learning_rate: float = 0.0\\n    loss_history: list = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    # Timing\\n    start_time: float = field(default_factory=time.time, init=False)\\n\\n    # Progress bar\\n    pbar: Optional[tqdm.tqdm] = field(default=None, repr=False, compare=False)\\n\\n    def start_tracker(self, console: Optional[Console] = None):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._start_rich_tracker(console)\\n        else:\\n            self._start_tqdm_tracker()\\n\\n    def _start_rich_tracker(self, console: Optional[Console] = None):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the rich progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self._console = _CONSOLE if console is None else console\\n\\n        # Create progress bar display\\n        self._progress = Progress(\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold blue]Training[/bold blue]\\"),\\n            BarColumn(bar_width=None),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[progress.percentage]{task.percentage:>3.0f}%\\"),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold white]•[/bold white]\\"),\\n            TextColumn(\\"Epoch {task.fields[epoch]}/{task.fields[total_epochs]}\\"),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold white]•[/bold white]\\"),\\n            TimeElapsedColumn(),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold white]•[/bold white]\\"),\\n            TimeRemainingColumn(),\\n            console=self._console,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Create stats display\\n        self._stats = Progress(\\n            TextColumn(\\"{task.description}\\"),\\n            console=self._console,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Add tasks\\n        self._task_id = self._progress.add_task(\\n            description=\\"\\",\\n            total=self.total_steps,\\n            completed=0,\\n            epoch=1,\\n            total_epochs=self.total_epochs,\\n        )\\n\\n        self._stats_task_id = self._stats.add_task(\\n            total=None,\\n            description=self._format_stats_text(),\\n        )\\n\\n        # Create Live display\\n        self._live = Live(\\n            Panel(\\n                self._progress,\\n                title=f\\"[bold]Fine-tuning {self.model}[/bold]\\",\\n                box=box.ROUNDED,\\n            ),\\n            console=self._console,\\n            refresh_per_second=4,\\n            transient=True,\\n        )\\n        self._live.start()\\n\\n    def _start_tqdm_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the tqdm progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self.pbar = tqdm.tqdm(\\n            total=self.total_steps,\\n            desc=f\\"Training {self.model}\\",\\n            unit=\\"step\\",\\n        )\\n        self._last_stats_update = time.time()\\n\\n    def _format_stats_text(self) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Format the stats text for display.\\"\\"\\"\\n        elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time\\n        steps_per_sec = self.current_step / max(0.001, elapsed)\\n        tokens_per_sec = self.tokens_processed / max(0.001, elapsed)\\n\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            return (\\n                f\\"[bold white]Loss:[/bold white] [yellow]{self.current_loss:.4f}[/yellow] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]LR:[/bold white] [blue]{self.learning_rate:.2e}[/blue] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]Steps/sec:[/bold white] [green]{steps_per_sec:.2f}[/green] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]Tokens/sec:[/bold white] [green]{tokens_per_sec:.0f}[/green] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] \\"\\n                f\\"[bold white]Samples:[/bold white] [blue]{self.samples_processed}[/blue]\\"\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            return (\\n                f\\"Loss: {self.current_loss:.4f} | \\"\\n                f\\"LR: {self.learning_rate:.2e} | \\"\\n                f\\"Steps/sec: {steps_per_sec:.2f} | \\"\\n                f\\"Tokens/sec: {tokens_per_sec:.0f} | \\"\\n                f\\"Samples: {self.samples_processed}\\"\\n            )\\n\\n    def update(self, stats: TrainingStats):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the tracker with new training stats.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self.current_epoch = stats.current_epoch\\n        self.current_step = stats.current_step\\n        self.current_loss = stats.current_loss\\n        self.tokens_processed = stats.tokens_processed\\n        self.samples_processed = stats.samples_processed\\n        self.learning_rate = stats.learning_rate\\n\\n        if stats.current_loss > 0:\\n            self.loss_history.append(stats.current_loss)\\n\\n        self._update_display()\\n\\n    def _update_display(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the progress display.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._progress.update(\\n                self._task_id,\\n                completed=self.current_step,\\n                epoch=self.current_epoch,\\n                total_epochs=self.total_epochs,\\n            )\\n            self._stats.update(\\n                self._stats_task_id,\\n                description=self._format_stats_text(),\\n            )\\n\\n            # Update the panel to include stats\\n            self._live.update(\\n                Panel(\\n                    f\\"{self._progress}\\\\n{self._stats}\\",\\n                    title=f\\"[bold]Fine-tuning {self.model}[/bold]\\",\\n                    box=box.ROUNDED,\\n                )\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.n = self.current_step\\n                self.pbar.set_description(\\n                    f\\"Training {MODEL}{self.model}{END} \\" f\\"[Epoch {self.current_epoch}/{self.total_epochs} | \\" f\\"Loss: {METRIC}{self.current_loss:.4f}{END}]\\"\\n                )\\n                self.pbar.refresh()\\n\\n    def stop_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Stop the tracker and display final statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n                self._live.stop()\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.close()\\n\\n        self.display_final_stats()\\n\\n    def display_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final training statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time\\n        avg_loss = sum(self.loss_history) / len(self.loss_history) if self.loss_history else 0.0\\n        final_loss = self.loss_history[-1] if self.loss_history else 0.0\\n\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._display_rich_final_stats(elapsed, avg_loss, final_loss)\\n        else:\\n            self._display_simple_final_stats(elapsed, avg_loss, final_loss)\\n\\n    def _display_rich_final_stats(self, elapsed: float, avg_loss: float, final_loss: float):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final stats using rich table.\\"\\"\\"\\n        table = Table(title=\\"Fine-tuning Complete\\", box=box.ROUNDED)\\n        table.add_column(\\"Metric\\", style=\\"cyan\\")\\n        table.add_column(\\"Value\\", style=\\"yellow\\")\\n\\n        table.add_row(\\"Model\\", f\\"[blue]{self.model}[/blue]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Epochs\\", str(self.total_epochs))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Steps\\", str(self.current_step))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Final Loss\\", f\\"[green]{final_loss:.4f}[/green]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Average Loss\\", f\\"{avg_loss:.4f}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Time\\", f\\"{elapsed:.2f}s\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Samples Processed\\", str(self.samples_processed))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Tokens Processed\\", f\\"{self.tokens_processed:,}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Steps/second\\", f\\"{self.current_step / max(0.001, elapsed):.2f}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Tokens/second\\", f\\"{self.tokens_processed / max(0.001, elapsed):.0f}\\")\\n\\n        self._console.print(table)\\n\\n    def _display_simple_final_stats(self, elapsed: float, avg_loss: float, final_loss: float):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final stats in plain text.\\"\\"\\"\\n        stats = [\\n            f\\"\\\\n{HEADER}Fine-tuning Complete{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Model: {MODEL}{self.model}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Epochs: {METRIC}{self.total_epochs}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Steps: {METRIC}{self.current_step}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Final Loss: {METRIC}{final_loss:.4f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Loss: {METRIC}{avg_loss:.4f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Time: {METRIC}{elapsed:.2f}s{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Samples Processed: {METRIC}{self.samples_processed}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Tokens Processed: {METRIC}{self.tokens_processed:,}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Steps/second: {METRIC}{self.current_step / max(0.001, elapsed):.2f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Tokens/second: {METRIC}{self.tokens_processed / max(0.001, elapsed):.0f}{END}\\",\\n        ]\\n        print(\\"\\\\n\\".join(stats))\\n\\n    def get_stats(self) -> TrainingStats:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get current training stats.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return TrainingStats(\\n            current_epoch=self.current_epoch,\\n            total_epochs=self.total_epochs,\\n            current_step=self.current_step,\\n            total_steps=self.total_steps,\\n            current_loss=self.current_loss,\\n            tokens_processed=self.tokens_processed,\\n            samples_processed=self.samples_processed,\\n            learning_rate=self.learning_rate,\\n            elapsed_time=time.time() - self.start_time,\\n        )\\n\\"\\"\\"Fine-tuning module for curator.\\n\\nThis module provides LoRA-based fine-tuning via the Tinker API (``TinkerTrainer``)\\nand managed fine-tuning via the Fireworks AI API (``FireworksTrainer``).\\n\\nExample usage:\\n    ```python\\n    from bespokelabs.curator import output_model=None, metrics_url=None):\\n        self.state = SimpleNamespace(name=state_name)\\n        self.output_model = output_model\\n        self.metrics_file_signed_url = metrics_url\\n        self.create_time = None\\n\\n\\nclass FakeJob:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Self-advancing fake fine-tuning job.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, states, output_model, display_name=\\"job\\"):\\n list(states)\\n        self._i = 0\\n        self._final_output = output_model\\n        initial = self._states[0]\\n        self._proto = FakeProto(initial, output_model if initial == \\"COMPLETED\\" else None)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def output_model(self):\\n        return self._proto.output_model\\n\\n    def get(self):\\n        self._i = min(self._i + 1, len(self._states) - 1)\\n        name = self._states[self._i]\\n        self._proto = FakeProto(name, self._final_output if name == \\"COMPLETED\\" else None)\\n        return self\\n\\n\\nclass FakeDataset:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Stand-in for fireworks.Dataset.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    last_path = None\\n\\n    def __init__(self, path=None):\\n        self.name = \\"accounts/mahesh/datasets/fake-dataset\\"\\n        self._path = path\\n        self.synced = False\\n\\n    @classmethod\\n    def from_file(cls, path):\\n        cls.last_path = path\\n        return cls(path)\\n\\n    def sync(self):\\n        self.synced = True\\n\\n\\nclass FakeLLM:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Stand-in for fireworks.LLM.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    last_init_kwargs = None\\n    last_sft_kwargs = None\\n\\n    def __init__(self, **kwargs):\\n        FakeLLM.last_init_kwargs = kwargs\\n        self.kwargs = kwargs\\n        self.model = kwargs.get(\\"model\\")\\n        self._gateway = SimpleNamespace(account_id=lambda: \\"mahesh\\", _api_key=kwargs.get(\\"api_key\\"))\\n        self.deployment_url = \\"https://app.fireworks.ai/deployment\\"\\n        self.applied = False\\n        self.deleted = False\\n\\n    def create_supervised_fine_tuning_job(self, display_name, dataset, **kwargs):\\n        FakeLLM.last_sft_kwargs = kwargs\\n        return FakeJob([\\"CREATING\\", \\"RUNNING\\", \\"COMPLETED\\"], \\"accounts/mahesh/models/curator-out\\", display_name)\\n\\n    def apply(self, wait=True):\\n        self.applied = True\\n        return self\\n\\n    def delete_deployment(self, ignore_checks=False, wait=True):\\n        self.deleted = True\\n\\n\\ndef install_fake_sdk(monkeypatch, llm_cls=FakeLLM):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Patch the trainer module to use the fake SDK.\\"\\"\\"\\n    fake_fireworks = SimpleNamespace(LLM=llm_cls, Dataset=FakeDataset)\\n Any import of\n``finetune.types`` or ``finetune.config`` sits under ``if TYPE_CHECKING:`` so\n``TrainingResult.step_plan: Optional[StepPlan]`` creates no import cycle.\n"""\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport math\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Mapping, Sequence, Tuple\n\nfrom xxhash import xxh64\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from bespokelabs.curator.finetune.config import TinkerTrainerConfig\n    from bespokelabs.curator.finetune.types import CheckpointInfo\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Constants\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nSTEP_UNIT_OPTIMIZER = "optimizer_step"\nSTEP_UNIT_PACKED = "packed_epoch_step"\n\nDATASET_SIGNATURE_PREFIX = "ds1"\n\nCHECKPOINT_REASONS: Tuple[str, str, str] = ("interval", "epoch", "final")\nCHECKPOINT_NAME_TEMPLATE = "{prefix}-s{step:06d}"\n\nMIN_LR_RATIO: float = 0.1\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Errors\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass StepLedgerError(ValueError):\n    """Raised for any malformed step plan or resume contract violation."""\n\n\nclass ResumePlanMismatch(StepLedgerError):\n    """Raised when a checkpoint cannot be resumed against the current plan.\n\n    Carries enough detail for a caller to report exactly which fields diverged\n    and what the plan/checkpoint each expected.\n    """\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        checkpoint_name: str,\n        mismatched_fields: Tuple[str, ...],\n        expected: dict,\n        found: dict,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.checkpoint_name = checkpoint_name\n        self.mismatched_fields = mismatched_fields\n        self.expected = expected\n        self.found = found\n        super().__init__(\n            f"Checkpoint {checkpoint_name!r} cannot be resumed: "\n            f"mismatched fields {mismatched_fields} "\n            f"(expected={expected!r}, found={found!r})"\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Frozen dataclasses\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass StepPlan:\n    """The immutable shape of a training run.\n\n    All step counts speak in optimizer steps. A batch is the unit of data\n    consumed per forward/backward; a step is the unit of optimizer updates.\n    """\n\n    step_unit: str\n    num_examples: int\n    batch_size: int\n    epochs: int\n    gradient_accumulation_steps: int\n    batches_per_epoch: int\n    total_batches: int\n    total_steps: int\n    trailing_window_batches: int\n    dataset_signature: str\n\n    # --- batch -> step helpers (1-based batch ordinals in) -----------------\n\n    def step_of_batch(self, b: int) -> int:\n        """The 1-based optimizer step that the ``b``-th global batch belongs to."""\n        return (b - 1) // self.gradient_accumulation_steps + 1\n\n    def is_step_boundary(self, b: int) -> int:  # noqa: FBT - trivial bool\n        """True iff the ``b``-th global batch closes an optimizer step."""\n        return b % self.gradient_accumulation_steps == 0 or b == self.total_batches\n\n    def epoch_of_batch(self, b: int) -> int:\n        """The 1-based epoch the ``b``-th global batch falls in."""\n        return (b - 1) // self.batches_per_epoch + 1\n\n    def batch_slice(self, b: int) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n        """The half-open ``[start, end)`` slice into the example list for batch ``b``."""\n        idx = b - 1\n        start = (idx % self.batches_per_epoch) * self.batch_size\n        end = min(start + self.batch_size, self.num_examples)\n        return start, end\n\n    def logging_steps(self, log_every_n_steps: int) -> Tuple[int, ...]:\n        """Optimizer steps at which to log: those divisible by ``n``."""\n        if log_every_n_steps < 1:\n            return tuple()\n        return tuple(s for s in range(1, self.total_steps + 1) if s % log_every_n_steps == 0)\n\n    def loss_history_steps(self, log_every_n_steps: int) -> Tuple[int, ...]:\n        """The ascending, deduplicated union of logging steps and the step that\n        closes the window containing each epoch\'s last batch."""\n        steps = set(self.logging_steps(log_every_n_steps))\n        for epoch in range(1, self.epochs + 1):\n            last_batch = epoch * self.batches_per_epoch\n            steps.add(self.step_of_batch(last_batch))\n        return tuple(sorted(steps))\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ResumePlan:\n    """Where a resumed run picks up, validated against a checkpoint\'s shape."""\n\n    checkpoint_name: str\n    start_batch_ordinal: int  # 0-based: the next batch to run is ``b0 + 1``\n    start_epoch: int\n    start_batch_in_epoch: int\n    completed_steps: int\n    remaining_batches: int\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Step planning\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef plan_steps(\n    num_examples: int,\n    *,\n    batch_size: int,\n    epochs: int,\n    gradient_accumulation_steps: int = 1,\n    dataset_signature: str = "",\n) -> StepPlan:\n    """Build a :class:`StepPlan` for the standard (Tinker) optimizer-step unit.\n\n    ``total_steps`` counts optimizer steps over the *whole run*: the accumulation\n    window is not reset at an epoch boundary, so a window may span one.\n    """\n    if num_examples == 0:\n        raise StepLedgerError("num_examples must be greater than 0")\n    if batch_size < 1:\n        raise StepLedgerError("batch_size must be >= 1")\n    if epochs < 1:\n        raise StepLedgerError("epochs must be >= 1")\n    if gradient_accumulation_steps < 1:\n        raise StepLedgerError("gradient_accumulation_steps must be >= 1")\n\n    batches_per_epoch = math.ceil(num_examples / batch_size)\n    total_batches = batches_per_epoch * epochs\n    total_steps = math.ceil(total_batches / gradient_accumulation_steps)\n    trailing_window_batches = total_batches - (total_steps - 1) * gradient_accumulation_steps\n\n    return StepPlan(\n        step_unit=STEP_UNIT_OPTIMIZER,\n        num_examples=num_examples,\n        batch_size=batch_size,\n        epochs=epochs,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=gradient_accumulation_steps,\n        batches_per_epoch=batches_per_epoch,\n        total_batches=total_batches,\n        total_steps=total_steps,\n        trailing_window_batches=trailing_window_batches,\n        dataset_signature=dataset_signature,\n    )\n\n\ndef plan_steps_for_config(config: "TinkerTrainerConfig", examples: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> StepPlan:\n    """Build a :class:`StepPlan` from a config and the example list."""\n    return plan_steps(\n        len(examples),\n        batch_size=config.batch_size,\n        epochs=config.epochs,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=config.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n        dataset_signature=dataset_signature(examples),\n    )\n\n\ndef plan_packed_steps(num_examples: int, *, epochs: int, dataset_signature: str = "") -> StepPlan:\n    """Build a :class:`StepPlan` for the Fireworks packed-epoch unit.\n\n    Fireworks packs all examples into a single packed batch per epoch, so one\n    optimizer step == one epoch.\n    """\n    if num_examples == 0:\n        raise StepLedgerError("num_examples must be greater than 0")\n    if epochs < 1:\n        raise StepLedgerError("epochs must be >= 1")\n\n    return StepPlan(\n        step_unit=STEP_UNIT_PACKED,\n        num_examples=num_examples,\n        batch_size=num_examples,\n        epochs=epochs,\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=1,\n        batches_per_epoch=1,\n        total_batches=epochs,\n        total_steps=epochs,\n        trailing_window_batches=1,\n        dataset_signature=dataset_signature,\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Dataset signature\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef dataset_signature(examples: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:\n    """A content-addressed signature of the ordered example list.\n\n    ``f"{DATASET_SIGNATURE_PREFIX}-{len(examples)}-{xxh64(payload).hexdigest()}"``\n    where ``payload`` is a canonical JSON encoding of the examples. It is a\n    function of the content of every example in order: reordering or editing\n    one character changes it; dict key order does not.\n    """\n    payload = json.dumps(\n        list(examples),\n        sort_keys=True,\n        separators=(",", ":"),\n        default=str,\n        ensure_ascii=True,\n    ).encode("utf-8")\n    return f"{DATASET_SIGNATURE_PREFIX}-{len(examples)}-{xxh64(payload).hexdigest()}"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Learning rate\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef learning_rate_at(\n    step: int,\n    total_steps: int,\n    base_lr: float,\n    warmup_steps: int,\n    *,\n    min_lr_ratio: float = MIN_LR_RATIO,\n) -> float:\n    """Linear warmup then linear decay to ``base_lr * min_lr_ratio``.\n\n    Unlike the legacy helper this *does* depend on ``total_steps``. ``warmup_steps``\n    greater than ``total_steps`` is clipped to the run length rather than raising.\n    Beyond the end of the run the rate is clamped at the floor.\n    """\n    if total_steps < 1:\n        total_steps = 1\n    effective_warmup = min(warmup_steps, total_steps)\n\n    if step <= effective_warmup:\n        if effective_warmup == 0:\n            # No warmup at all: start at the decay schedule for step 1.\n            pass\n        else:\n            return base_lr * step / effective_warmup\n\n    # Decay phase (also reached when warmup is 0 or step is past warmup).\n    progress = min((step - effective_warmup) / max(total_steps - effective_warmup, 1), 1.0)\n    if progress > 1.0:\n        progress = 1.0\n    lr = base_lr * (1.0 - (1.0 - min_lr_ratio) * progress)\n    floor = base_lr * min_lr_ratio\n    if lr < floor:\n        lr = floor\n    return lr\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Checkpoint naming / reasons\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef checkpoint_name(prefix: str, step: int) -> str:\n    """Derive a deterministic checkpoint name from a prefix and optimizer step."""\n    return CHECKPOINT_NAME_TEMPLATE.format(prefix=prefix, step=step)\n\n\ndef canonical_reasons(reasons: Sequence[str]) -> Tuple[str, ...]:\n    """Deduplicate ``reasons`` and order them by :data:`CHECKPOINT_REASONS`."""\n    unknown = [r for r in reasons if r not in CHECKPOINT_REASONS]\n    if unknown:\n        raise StepLedgerError(\n            f"Unknown checkpoint reasons {unknown!r}; expected subset of {CHECKPOINT_REASONS!r}"\n        )\n    present = set(reasons)\n    return tuple(r for r in CHECKPOINT_REASONS if r in present)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Resume\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef plan_resume(plan: StepPlan, checkpoint: "CheckpointInfo") -> ResumePlan:\n    """Validate a checkpoint against a plan and compute the resume position.\n\n    Validates in a fixed order and raises :class:`ResumePlanMismatch` on the\n    first failure:\n\n    1. **shape** -- ``batch_size``, ``dataset_signature`` and\n       ``gradient_accumulation_steps`` against the plan; every differing name\n       goes into ``mismatched_fields``, sorted alphabetically. A pre-ledger\n       checkpoint (all three zero/empty) fails all three.\n    2. **exhaustion** -- the checkpoint already covers the whole run\n       (``batches_completed >= plan.total_batches``) reports ``("epochs",)``.\n    3. **consistency** -- ``checkpoint.step`` must equal the step of the\n       batch after which it was taken; otherwise ``("step",)``.\n\n    Otherwise the run restarts at the batch *after* the checkpoint. Raising\n    ``epochs`` alone is a legal resume: the plan grows and the schedule\n    continues from ``completed_steps + 1``.\n    """\n    # 1. shape\n    shape_fields = (\n        "batch_size",\n        "dataset_signature",\n        "gradient_accumulation_steps",\n    )\n    mismatched: list = []\n    expected: dict = {}\n    found: dict = {}\n    for field_name in shape_fields:\n        plan_value = getattr(plan, field_name)\n        ckpt_value = getattr(checkpoint, field_name)\n        if ckpt_value != plan_value:\n            mismatched.append(field_name)\n            expected[field_name] = plan_value\n            found[field_name] = ckpt_value\n    if mismatched:\n        mismatched_sorted = tuple(sorted(mismatched))\n        raise ResumePlanMismatch(\n            checkpoint_name=checkpoint.name,\n            mismatched_fields=mismatched_sorted,\n            expected=expected,\n            found=found,\n        )\n\n    # 2. exhaustion\n    if checkpoint.batches_completed >= plan.total_batches:\n        raise ResumePlanMismatch(\n            checkpoint_name=checkpoint.name,\n            mismatched_fields=("epochs",),\n            expected={"epochs": plan.epochs},\n            found={"batches_completed": checkpoint.batches_completed},\n        )\n\n    # 3. consistency\n    expected_step = plan.step_of_batch(checkpoint.batches_completed)\n    if checkpoint.step != expected_step:\n        raise ResumePlanMismatch(\n            checkpoint_name=checkpoint.name,\n            mismatched_fields=("step",),\n            expected={"step": expected_step},\n            found={"step": checkpoint.step},\n        )\n\n    start_batch_ordinal = checkpoint.batches_completed  # 0-based: next batch is +1\n    start_epoch = checkpoint.batches_completed // plan.batches_per_epoch + 1\n    start_batch_in_epoch = checkpoint.batches_completed % plan.batches_per_epoch\n    completed_steps = checkpoint.step\n    remaining_batches = plan.total_batches - checkpoint.batches_completed\n\n    return ResumePlan(\n        checkpoint_name=checkpoint.name,\n        start_batch_ordinal=start_batch_ordinal,\n        start_epoch=start_epoch,\n        start_batch_in_epoch=start_batch_in_epoch,\n        completed_steps=completed_steps,\n        remaining_batches=remaining_batches,\n    )\n'}

Build the plan first: one honest source of totals.\n        plan = plan_steps(\n            len(data_list),\n            batch_size=self.config.batch_size,\n            epochs=self.config.epochs,\n            gradient_accumulation_steps=self.config.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n            dataset_signature=dataset_signature(data_list),\n        )\n\n        # Accumulate per-batch losses within the current optimizer-step window so\n        # each loss_history entry is the mean of its window\'s per-batch losses.\n        window_losses: List[float] = []\n        completed_steps = resume.completed_steps if resume is not None else 0\n\n        # The next batch to run is the one after the checkpoint (1-based).\n        start_b = (resume.start_batch_ordinal + 1) if resume is not None else 1\n\n        try:\n            for b in range(start_b, plan.total_batches + 1):\n                step = plan.step_of_batch(b)\n                lr = self._get_learning_rate(step, plan.total_steps)\n\n                start_idx, end_idx = plan.batch_slice(b)\n                batch = data_list[start_idx:end_idx]\n                batch_data = self._prepare_batch(batch)\n\n                should_optim_step = plan.is_step_boundary(b)\n                loss, batch_tokens = self._training_step(\n                    batch_data, learning_rate=lr, should_optim_step=should_optim_step\n                )\n\n                window_losses.append(loss)\n                tokens_processed += batch_tokens\n                samples_processed += len(batch)\n\n                if should_optim_step:\n                    completed_steps += 1\n\n                stats = TrainingStats(\n                    current_epoch=plan.epoch_of_batch(b),\n                    total_epochs=self.config.epochs,\n                    current_step=completed_steps,\n                    total_steps=plan.total_steps,\n                    current_loss=loss,\n                    tokens_processed=tokens_processed,\n                    samples_processed=samples_processed,\n                    learning_rate=lr,\n                    current_batch=b,\n                    total_batches=plan.total_batches,\n                )\n                status_tracker.update(stats)\n\n                # Checkpoints are written only at optimizer-step boundaries. A step\n                # that fires several triggers produces exactly one save_checkpoint\n                # call whose reasons is the canonical tuple of all fired triggers.\n                if should_optim_step:\n                    reasons: List[str] = []\n                    if self.config.checkpoint_every_n_steps > 0 and step % self.config.checkpoint_every_n_steps == 0:\n                        reasons.append("interval")\n                    if self.config.checkpoint_every_epoch and b % plan.batches_per_epoch == 0:\n                        reasons.append("epoch")\n                    if step == plan.total_steps:\n                        reasons.append("final")\n                    if reasons:\n                        ckpt_name = checkpoint_name(self.config.checkpoint_name_prefix, step)\n                        epoch_for_ckpt = plan.epoch_of_batch(b)\n                        self.save_checkpoint(\n                            name=ckpt_name,\n                            step=step,\n                            epoch=epoch_for_ckpt,\n                            loss=loss,\n                            batch_size=plan.batch_size,\n                            gradient_accumulation_steps=plan.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n                            batches_completed=b,\n                            dataset_signature=plan.dataset_signature,\n                            reasons=reasons,\n                        )\n\n        finally:\n            status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n\n        total_time = self._clock() - start_time\n        final_loss = loss_history[-1] if loss_history else 0.0\n\n        self._is_trained = True\n\n        if self.config.save_weights_on_complete:\n            # A fresh clock reading for the auto-name, never total_time\'s value.\n            self._weights_name = self.save_weights(f"{self.config.base_model}_lora_{int(self._clock())}")\n\n        metadata = {\n            "base_model": self.config.base_model,\n            "batch_size": self.config.batch_size,\n            "learning_rate": self.config.adam_params.learning_rate,\n            "lora_rank": self.config.lora_config.rank,\n            "lora_alpha": self.config.lora_config.alpha,\n            "gradient_accumulation_steps": self.config.gradient_accumulation_steps,\n            "dataset_signature": plan.dataset_signature,\n        }\n\n        result = TrainingResult(\n            final_loss=final_loss,\n            total_steps=plan.total_steps,\n            total_epochs=self.config.epochs,\n            total_time=total_time,\n            tokens_processed=tokens_processed,\n            samples_processed=samples_processed,\n            loss_history=loss_history,\n            weights_name=self._weights_name,\n            checkpoints=self._checkpoints.copy(),\n            metadata=metadata,\n            total_batches=plan.total_batches,\n            step_plan=plan,\n        )\n\n        logger.info(f"Training complete. Determine resume point from a previously loaded checkpoint\n        resume_step = self._resume_from_step\n        resume_epoch = self._resume_from_epoch\n        # Reset so subsequent train() calls without load_checkpoint start fresh\n        self._resume_from_step = 0\n        self._resume_from_epoch = 0\n\n        if resume_step > 0:\n            logger.info(\n                f"Resuming training from step {resume_step} (epoch {resume_epoch}): "\n                f"{num_examples} examples, {self.config.epochs} epochs, {total_steps} total steps"\n            )\n        else:\n            logger.info(f"Starting training: {num_examples} examples, {self.config.epochs} epochs, {total_steps} total steps")\n\n        # Compute loop start bounds from resume state (cookbook pattern:\n        # adjust loop bounds rather than skipping inside loops)\n        if resume_step > 0:\n            steps_before_resume_epoch = (resume_epoch - 1) * steps_per_epoch\n            resume_batch_offset = resume_step - steps_before_resume_epoch\n            if resume_batch_offset >= steps_per_epoch:\n                # Entire epoch was completed (e.g. end-of-epoch checkpoint)\n                start_epoch = resume_epoch + 1\n                start_batch_idx = 0\n            else:\n                start_epoch = resume_epoch\n                start_batch_idx = resume_batch_offset\n        else:\n            start_epoch = 1\n            start_batch_idx = 0\n\n        current_step = resume_step\n\n        try:\n            for epoch in range(start_epoch, self.config.epochs + 1):\n                epoch_loss = 0.0\n                epoch_steps = 0\n\n                # On the first resumed epoch, skip already-completed batches;\n                # subsequent epochs start from 0\n                first_batch = start_batch_idx if epoch == start_epoch else 0\n                batch_positions = list(range(0, num_examples, self.config.batch_size))\n\n                for batch_start in batch_positions[first_batch:]:\n                    batch_end = min(batch_start + self.config.batch_size, num_examples)\n                    batch = data_list[batch_start:batch_end]\n\n                    current_step += 1\n                    lr = self._get_learning_rate(current_step, total_steps)\n                    batch_data = self._prepare_batch(batch)\n                    # Only step the optimizer after accumulating enough gradients\n                    should_optim_step = current_step % self.config.gradient_accumulation_steps == 0\n                    loss, batch_tokens = self._training_step(batch_data, learning_rate=lr, should_optim_step=should_optim_step)\n\n                    epoch_loss += loss\n                    epoch_steps += 1\n                    tokens_processed += batch_tokens\n                    samples_processed += len(batch)\n\n                    stats = TrainingStats(\n                        current_epoch=epoch,\n                        total_epochs=self.config.epochs,\n                        current_step=current_step,\n                        total_steps=total_steps,\n                        current_loss=loss,\n                        tokens_processed=tokens_processed,\n                        samples_processed=samples_processed,\n                        learning_rate=lr,\n                    )\n                    status_tracker.update(stats)\n\n                    if current_step % self.config.log_every_n_steps == 0:\n                        avg_loss = epoch_loss / epoch_steps\n                        loss_history.append(avg_loss)\n                        logger.debug(f"Step {current_step}/{total_steps}, Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, LR: {lr:.2e}")\n\n                    if self.config.checkpoint_every_n_steps > 0 and current_step % self.config.checkpoint_every_n_steps == 0:\n                        checkpoint_name = f"{self.config.checkpoint_name_prefix}_step_{current_step}"\n                        self.save_checkpoint(\n                            name=checkpoint_name,\n                            step=current_step,\n                            epoch=epoch,\n                            loss=loss,\n                        )\n\n                if epoch_steps > 0:\n                    avg_epoch_loss = epoch_loss / epoch_steps\n                    # Only append if the last step wasn\'t already logged by per-step logging\n                    if current_step % self.config.log_every_n_steps != 0:\n                        loss_history.append(avg_epoch_loss)\n                    logger.info(f"Epoch {epoch}/{self.config.epochs} complete. Average loss: {avg_epoch_loss:.4f}")\n                else:\n                    avg_epoch_loss = 0.0\n                    logger.info(f"Epoch {epoch}/{self.config.epochs} complete. No steps executed.")\n\n                if self.config.checkpoint_every_epoch:\n                    checkpoint_name = f"{self.config.checkpoint_name_prefix}_epoch_{epoch}"\n                    self.save_checkpoint(\n                        name=checkpoint_name,\n                        step=current_step,\n                        epoch=epoch,\n                        loss=avg_epoch_loss,\n                    )\n\n            # Flush any remaining accumulated gradients from a partial window\n            if (\n                self.config.gradient_accumulation_steps > 1\n                and current_step % self.config.gradient_accumulation_steps != 0\n                and self._training_client is not None\n                and TINKER_AVAILABLE\n            ):\n                try:\n                    lr = self._get_learning_rate(current_step, total_steps)\n                    adam_params = tinker.AdamParams(\n                        learning_rate=lr,\n                        beta1=self.config.adam_params.beta1,\n                        beta2=self.config.adam_params.beta2,\n                        eps=self.config.adam_params.epsilon,\n                        weight_decay=self.config.adam_params.weight_decay,\n                    )\n                    self._training_client.optim_step(adam_params).result()\n                except Exception as e:\n                    logger.warning(f"Failed to flush final gradient accumulation: {e}")\n\n        finally:\n            status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n\n        total_time = time.time() - start_time\n        final_loss = loss_history[-1] if loss_history else 0.0\n\n        self._is_trained = True\n\n        if self.config.save_weights_on_complete:\n            self._weights_name = self.save_weights(f"{self.config.base_model}_lora_{int(time.time())}")\n\n        result = TrainingResult(\n            final_loss=final_loss,\n            total_steps=current_step,\n            total_epochs=self.config.epochs,\n            total_time=total_time,\n            tokens_processed=tokens_processed,\n            samples_processed=samples_processed,\n            loss_history=loss_history,\n            weights_name=self._weights_name,\n            checkpoints=self._checkpoints.copy(),\n            metadata={\n                "base_model": self.config.base_model,\n                "batch_size": self.config.batch_size,\n                "learning_rate": self.config.adam_params.learning_rate,\n                "lora_rank": self.config.lora_config.rank,\n                "lora_alpha": self.config.lora_config.alpha,\n            },\n        )\n\n        logger.info(f"Training complete. The LR assignment is wrong. The LR for a batch must be the LR of the **step that batch belongs to**, but I'm computing `lr` from `step = plan.step_of_batch(b)` which is correct (step 1 for batches 1-3). The issue is `lr = self._get_learning_rate(step, plan.total_steps)` should give step-1 lr. But the values show batch 4 has lr=1e-4 when step=1. That's because batch 4 belongs to step 2! Let me recheck: batches 1,2,3 → step 1; batches 4,5,6 → step 2; batches 7,8 → step 3. So batch 4 should get step-2's lr = 1e-4. Correct!

The LR values are right. The `current_step` issue: I increment `completed_steps` after `_training_step` but the stats should reflect optimizer steps **completed so far** — which means the step that just closed. The spec says `current_step` = optimizer steps completed so far, flat inside a window. So batches 1,2 (not boundaries) → 0 completed; batch 3 (boundary, step 1 completes) → 1; batches 4,5 → 1; batch 6 → 2; batch 7 → 2; batch 8 → 3. My output shows exactly that: `[0,0,1,1,1,2,2,3]`. 

So my assertion was wrong, the actual behavior is correct per spec. Let me verify the LR values: batches 1-3 → step1 lr 5e-05; 4-6 → step2 lr 1e-04; 7-8 → step3 lr 1e-05. Output shows: `5e-05, 5e-05, 5e-05, 1e-04, 1e-04, 1e-04, 1e-05, 1e-05`. Correct! Clock calls = 2.
