The job may be rejected.")\n\n        if self.is_mock:\n            return self._mock_train(num_examples, start_time)\n\n        # Reset all trained state up front so a failed (re)train leaves the trainer in a\n        # clean "not trained" state instead of silently retaining the previous run\'s\n        # model. Also tear down any deployment of the now-stale previous model.\n        self._is_trained = False\n        self._output_model = None\n        self._job_name = None\n        self._job_url = None\n        self.delete_deployment()\n\n        logger.info(f"Starting Fireworks SFT: {num_examples} examples, {self.config.epochs} epochs, base model {self.config.qualified_base_model}")\n\n        tmp_path = self._write_dataset_file(examples)\n        try:\n            dataset_obj = fireworks.Dataset.from_file(tmp_path)\n            dataset_obj.sync()\n            logger.info(f"Dataset uploaded: {dataset_obj.name}")\n\n            job = self._create_job(dataset_obj)\n            self._job_name = getattr(job, "name", None)\n            self._job_url = getattr(job, "url", None)\n            logger.info(f"Fine-tuning job submitted: {self._job_name} ({self._job_url})")\n\n            job = self._poll_job(job)\n            self._output_model = job.output_model\n            logger.info(f"Fine-tuning complete. Now let me double check one subtle thing: the `loss_history` should have exactly one entry per step in `plan.loss_history_steps(...)`. For the e2e with log_every_n_steps=10 (default) and 3 total steps: logging_steps(10) = steps divisible by 10 in [1..3] = {} (none). Plus epoch-closing steps: epoch1 last batch=4 → step 2; epoch2 last batch=8 → step 3. So loss_history_steps = {2, 3} → (2, 3). Two entries. My e2e run showed 2 loss entries.

## Summary

I implemented the step ledger behind one new leaf module and rewired both trainers around it. Here's what changed:

### New module: `src/bespokelabs/curator/finetune/step_ledger.py`
A true leaf â imports only `json`, `math`, `dataclasses`, `typing`, and `xxhash`; no `time`/`datetime`/`random`, and `finetune.types`/`finetune.config` imports sit under `if TYPE_CHECKING:` so `TrainingResult.step_plan: Optional[StepPlan]` creates no cycle.

- **Constants**: `STEP_UNIT_OPTIMIZER`, `STEP_UNIT_PACKED`, `DATASET_SIGNATURE_PREFIX`, `CHECKPOINT_REASONS`, `CHECKPOINT_NAME_TEMPLATE`, `MIN_LR_RATIO`.
- **Exceptions**: `StepLedgerError(ValueError)`, `ResumePlanMismatch` (carries `checkpoint_name`, `mismatched_fields`, `expected`, `found`).
- **Frozen dataclasses**: `StepPlan` and `ResumePlan` with the exact field order specified.
- `plan_steps` â counts `total_steps` in **optimizer steps** over the whole run (window not reset at epoch boundaries); `trailing_window_batches` counts the short final window as one step. Methods: `step_of_batch`, `is_step_boundary`, `epoch_of_batch`, `batch_slice`, `logging_steps`, `loss_history_steps` (logging steps âª each epoch's closing step). Raises `StepLedgerError` on bad inputs.
- `plan_steps_for_config`, `plan_packed_steps` (Fireworks packed-epoch unit).
- `dataset_signature` â `ds1-{len}-{xxh64(canonical json)}`, content + order sensitive, key-order insensitive.
- `learning_rate_at` â linear warmup (clipped to run length) then linear decay to `base_lr * MIN_LR_RATIO`, clamped at the floor past the end.
- `checkpoint_name`, `canonical_reasons` (dedupes/orders by `CHECKPOINT_REASONS`, raises on unknown).
- `plan_resume` â validates shape â exhaustion â consistency in fixed order, raises `ResumePlanMismatch`; raising `epochs` alone is a legal resume.

### `types.py`
`CheckpointInfo` appended with `batch_size`, `gradient_accumulation_steps`, `batches_completed`, `dataset_signature`, `reasons` (length 10). `TrainingStats` gained `current_batch`, `total_batches`. `TrainingResult` gained `total_batches`, `step_plan`. All defaulted so existing construction sites keep working.

### `config.py`
`seed: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)` added to both `TinkerTrainerConfig` and `FireworksTrainerConfig`.

### `TinkerTrainer`
- Builds the plan first; walks batches by global 1-based ordinal `b`; steps the optimizer only at `plan.is_step_boundary(b)` â so the trailing window flushes **inside** the loop (mock mode too), deleting the post-loop `TINKER_AVAILABLE`-guarded flush.
- One `TrainingStats` per batch with `current_step` = optimizer steps completed (flat inside a window), correct per-window LR, and optimizer-step totals.
- `loss_history`: one entry per `plan.loss_history_steps(...)`, each the mean of its window's per-batch losses; `final_loss = loss_history[-1]`.
- `metadata` gains exactly `gradient_accumulation_steps` and `dataset_signature`.
- `save_checkpoint` records the run shape; `checkpoint_every_n_steps`/`checkpoint_every_epoch` speak in optimizer steps; the `epoch` trigger fires at the step closing the window containing an epoch's last batch; `final` fires at `step == total_steps`; one `save_checkpoint` per step with canonical merged reasons; repeat name replaces in place.
- `load_checkpoint` stores the whole `CheckpointInfo` on `self._resume_from`; `train()` validates via `plan_resume` (raises `ResumePlanMismatch`, never silent) and clears it so a second `train()` starts fresh.
- Determinism: keyword-only `clock`/`rng`; mock draws one `self._rng.random()` per batch (`2.5 - draw*0.5`), no `time.sleep`; `self._clock()` exactly twice normally, a third fresh reading for the auto-name under `save_weights_on_complete`.
- `_get_learning_rate` delegates to `learning_rate_at`.

### `FireworksTrainer`
Keyword-only `clock`/`rng`; `train` and `_mock_train` build `plan_packed_steps` and set `total_steps`/`total_batches`/`step_plan`; both metadata dicts gain `"step_unit": "packed_epoch_step"`; mock draws one rng and no `time.sleep`. Real-SDK `time.time`/`time.sleep` paths unchanged.

All 89 existing finetune tests pass, and all the r1/r2 observability assertions hold exactly.
